, then

\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{\mathcal{A}})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}}-\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}}\right\|_{\infty}\leq\kappa\omega\exp(O(L))\frac{(\log d)^{3L+1}}{\sqrt{d}}

holds for all
l\in[L]
and
\alpha\in[d_{0}]
with probability at least
1-\delta
.

Proof.

We have a similar decomposition as the previous lemma, which shows

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{\mathcal{A}})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)}-\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial h_{0}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\kappa\omega\frac{(\log d)^{3/2}}{\sqrt{d}}\exp(O(L))+C\log d\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{\mathcal{A}})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l+1)}(x)}-\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l+1)}(x)}\right\|_{\infty},

\displaystyle\left\|\ref{eqn:eqn28}\right\|_{\infty}\leq M_{2}\kappa\omega\exp(O(L))\frac{(\log d)^{2L+1/2}}{\sqrt{d}},

\displaystyle\left\|\ref{eqn:eqn29}\right\|_{\infty}\leq M_{2}\kappa\omega^{2}\exp(O(L))\frac{(\log d)^{4L}}{\sqrt{d}},

\displaystyle\left\|\ref{eqn:eqn30}\right\|_{\infty}\leq M_{2}\kappa\omega\frac{(\log d)^{2L+1/2}}{\sqrt{d}}\exp(O(L)),

\displaystyle\left\|\ref{eqn:eqn31}\right\|_{\infty}\leq M_{3}\kappa\omega\frac{(\log d)^{L+1}}{\sqrt{d}}\exp(O(L)),

\displaystyle\left\|\ref{eqn:eqn32}\right\|_{\infty}\leq M_{1}\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{\mathcal{A}})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}}-\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial h_{0}^{(l)}}\right\|_{\infty},

\displaystyle\left\|\ref{eqn:eqn33}\right\|_{\infty}\leq M_{2}\kappa\omega\frac{(\log d)^{L+1/2}}{\sqrt{d}}\exp(O(L)).

∎

As we’ve done in the previous section, we also give an asymptotic summary of the results.
If the width
d\geq\Omega(L\log(d_{0})/\delta)
, and the weights after perturbation satisfy
\|W_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}-W_{0}^{(l)}\|\leq\omega\sqrt{d}
and
\|W_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}-W_{0}^{(l)}\|_{\infty}\leq\omega\log d
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}-h_{0}^{(l)}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\omega\sqrt{d}O(\exp(L)),

(47)

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}}-\frac{h_{0}^{(l)}(x)}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\omega\sqrt{d\log d}\exp(O(L)),

(48)

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x)}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)}-\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\kappa\omega\exp(O(L))\sqrt{\log d},

(49)

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{\mathcal{A}})(x)}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)}-\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\kappa\omega\log d\exp(O(L))

(50)

for all
l\in[L]
and
\alpha\in[d_{0}]
simultaneously, with probability at least
1-\delta
.

I.3
Proof of Theorem
I.1

Now we are ready to prove Theorem
I.1
.

Proof.

We first recall the decomposition of finite JNTK to its layer-wise contributions,

\displaystyle\Theta_{d,\theta_{0}}(x,x^{\prime})_{00}

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{l=1}^{L+1}\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x),h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle,

\displaystyle\Theta_{d,\theta}(x,x^{\prime})_{\alpha 0}

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{l=1}^{L+1}\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x),h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l=1}^{L+1}\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle\frac{\partial h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x)}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}},h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle,

\displaystyle\Theta_{d,\theta}(x,x^{\prime})_{0\beta}

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{l=1}^{L+1}\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x),h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x^{\prime})_{\beta-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l=1}^{L+1}\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x),\frac{\partial h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})}{\partial x_{\beta-1}^{\prime}}\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle,

\displaystyle\Theta_{d,\theta}(x,x^{\prime})_{\alpha\beta}

\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{l=1}^{L+1}\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x),h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x^{\prime})_{\beta-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l=1}^{L+1}\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle\frac{\partial h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x)}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}},h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x^{\prime})_{\beta-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l=1}^{L+1}\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x),\frac{\partial h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})}{\partial x_{\beta-1}^{\prime}}\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l=1}^{L+1}\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle\frac{\partial h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x)}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}},\frac{\partial h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})}{\partial x_{\beta-1}^{\prime}}\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle.

We need to bound its difference
\Theta_{d,\theta_{\mathcal{A}}}(x,x^{\prime})
where
\theta_{\mathcal{A}}=\{W_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}\}_{l=1}^{L+1}
assuming that weights are close to their initialisation.

We will bound each summand in the above decomposition, which adds
\times(L+1)
multiplicative coefficient to the bound of finite JNTK difference.
By triangle inequality, we can bound each summands in the first term.

\displaystyle\Bigg|\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x),h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x),h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x)}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Bigg|\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x),h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x),h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle\Bigg|

\displaystyle+\Bigg|\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x),h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x),h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle\Bigg|

\displaystyle+\Bigg|\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x),h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x),h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x)}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle\Bigg|

\displaystyle+\Bigg|\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x),h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x)}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x),h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x)}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x)-h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)\right\|\left\|h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)\right\|\left\|h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})-h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)\right\|\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}-\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x)}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)\right\|\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x)}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}-\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|,

which rewrites the difference by the terms we’ve bounded in Appendices
I.1
and
I.2
.

We can bound the rest of the terms as follows:

\displaystyle\Bigg|\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x),h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x),h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial J(f_{\mathcal{A}})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x)-h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)\right\|\left\|h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)\right\|\left\|h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})-h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)\right\|\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}-\frac{\partial J(f_{\mathcal{A}})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)\right\|\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{\mathcal{A}})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}-\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|,

\displaystyle\Bigg|\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle\frac{\partial h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x)}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}},h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}},h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x)}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|\frac{\partial h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x)}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}}-\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}}\right\|\left\|h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}}\right\|\left\|h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})-h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}}\right\|\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}-\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x)}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}}\right\|\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x)}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}-\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|,

\displaystyle\Bigg|\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x),h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x^{\prime})_{\beta-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x),h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x)}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial J(f_{\mathcal{A}})(x^{\prime})_{\beta-1}}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x)-h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)\right\|\left\|h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x^{\prime})_{\beta-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)\right\|\left\|h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})-h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x^{\prime})_{\beta-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)\right\|\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}-\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x)}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x^{\prime})_{\beta-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)\right\|\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x)}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x^{\prime})_{\beta-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}-\frac{\partial J(f_{\mathcal{A}})(x^{\prime})_{\beta-1}}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|,

\displaystyle\Bigg|\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x),\frac{\partial h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})}{\partial x_{\beta-1}^{\prime}}\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x),\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})}{\partial x_{\beta-1}^{\prime}}\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x)}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x)-h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})}{\partial x_{\beta}^{\prime}}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})}{\partial x_{\beta}^{\prime}}-\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})}{\partial x_{\beta}^{\prime}}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})}{\partial x_{\beta}^{\prime}}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}-\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x)}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})}{\partial x_{\beta}^{\prime}}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x)}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}-\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|,

\displaystyle\Bigg|\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x),h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x^{\prime})_{\beta-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x),h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial J(f_{\mathcal{A}})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial J(f_{\mathcal{A}})(x^{\prime})_{\beta-1}}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x)-h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)\right\|\left\|h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x^{\prime})_{\beta-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)\right\|\left\|h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})-h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x^{\prime})_{\beta-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)\right\|\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}-\frac{\partial J(f_{\mathcal{A}})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x^{\prime})_{\beta-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)\right\|\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{\mathcal{A}})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x^{\prime})_{\beta-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}-\frac{\partial J(f_{\mathcal{A}})(x^{\prime})_{\beta-1}}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|,

\displaystyle\Bigg|\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle\frac{\partial h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x)}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}},h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x^{\prime})_{\beta-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}},h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x)}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial J(f_{\mathcal{A}})(x^{\prime})_{\beta-1}}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|\frac{\partial h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x)}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}}-\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}}\right\|\left\|h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x^{\prime})_{\beta-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}}\right\|\left\|h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})-h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x^{\prime})_{\beta-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}}\right\|\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}-\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x)}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x^{\prime})_{\beta-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}}\right\|\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x)}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x^{\prime})_{\beta-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}-\frac{\partial J(f_{\mathcal{A}})(x^{\prime})_{\beta-1}}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|,

\displaystyle\Bigg|\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x),\frac{\partial h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})}{\partial x_{\beta-1}^{\prime}}\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x),\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})}{\partial x_{\beta-1}^{\prime}}\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial J(f_{\mathcal{A}})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x)-h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})}{\partial x_{\beta}^{\prime}}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})}{\partial x_{\beta}^{\prime}}-\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})}{\partial x_{\beta}^{\prime}}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})}{\partial x_{\beta}^{\prime}}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{0})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}-\frac{\partial J(f_{\mathcal{A}})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})}{\partial x_{\beta}^{\prime}}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{\mathcal{A}})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}-\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|,

\displaystyle\Bigg|\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle\frac{\partial h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x)}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}},\frac{\partial h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})}{\partial x_{\beta-1}^{\prime}}\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\langle\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}},\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})}{\partial x_{\beta-1}^{\prime}}\right\rangle\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x)}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)},\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\rangle\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|\frac{\partial h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x)}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}}-\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})}{\partial x_{\beta}^{\prime}}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})}{\partial x_{\beta}^{\prime}}-\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})}{\partial x_{\beta}^{\prime}}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})}{\partial x_{\beta}^{\prime}}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x)}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x)}-\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x)}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{C_{l-1}}\left\|\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x)}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l-1)}(x^{\prime})}{\partial x_{\beta}^{\prime}}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x)}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{0}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}-\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x^{\prime})}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x^{\prime})}\right\|.

We will use the norm bounds for the activations and gradients after perturbation, which is asymptotically the same as norms before perturbations with assumptions on
\omega
.

\displaystyle\left\|h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{d}+\omega\sqrt{d}O(\exp(L))

\displaystyle\leq O(\exp(L))\sqrt{d},

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial h_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq O(\exp(L))\sqrt{d}+\omega\sqrt{d}O(\exp(L))

\displaystyle\leq O(\exp(L))\sqrt{d},

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial f_{\mathcal{A}}(x)}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\kappa O(\exp(L))+\kappa\omega\exp(O(L))\sqrt{\log d}

\displaystyle\leq\kappa\exp(O(L)),

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{\mathcal{A}})(x)_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{\mathcal{A}}^{(l)}(x)}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\kappa\exp(O(L))+\kappa\omega\log d\exp(O(L))+\kappa^{2}\omega^{2}\exp(O(L))(\log d)^{3/2}

\displaystyle\leq\kappa\exp(O(L)).

Combining these with Equation (
37
)-(
40
) and (
47
)-(
50
), we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\Theta_{d,\theta_{\mathcal{A}}}(x,x^{\prime})_{ij}-\Theta_{d,\theta_{0}}(x,x^{\prime})_{ij}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\kappa^{2}\omega\exp(O(L))\log d

for any
i,j\in\{0\}\cup[d_{0}]
.
We finally compute the bound on Frobenius norm as follows:

\left\|\Theta_{d,\theta_{\mathcal{A}}}(x,x^{\prime})-\Theta_{d,\theta_{0}}(x,x^{\prime})\right\|_{F}\leq\kappa^{2}\omega d_{0}\exp(O(L))\log d.

∎

Appendix J
Proof of Theorem
4.5

We now prove that the finite JNTK stays constant during the gradient flow.
We consider the weight to evolve along the gradient flow of the objective
\mathcal{L}
,

\dot{\theta}_{t}=-\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\theta_{t})}{\theta_{t}}

where we write each layer’s weights as
\theta_{t}=\{W^{(l)}(t)\}_{l=1}^{L+1}
.

As stated in Assumption
4.4
, we will assume that the smallest eigenvalue of the matrix
\Theta(x^{(1:N)},x^{(1:N)})
is greater than 0, with its value
\lambda_{0}
.
The proof of this theorem is based on two lemmas, whose condition and the results are reversed.

Lemma J.1
.

First, assume that the width network
d
is large enough to satisfy

d\geq F_{1}\left(x^{(1:N)},L,\delta,\frac{\epsilon}{\kappa}\right),d\geq F_{2}\left(x^{(1:N)},L,\delta,\epsilon\right)

where
F_{1}
is defined in Remark
E.1
and the
F_{2}
is defined in Remark
H.1
.

Further assume that the network width
d
and
\omega
satisfy

\displaystyle d

\displaystyle\geq\Omega(Nd_{0}\exp(L)/\delta),

\displaystyle\omega

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\frac{\lambda_{0}\lambda}{N\exp(\Omega(L))\log d}\right),

\displaystyle\omega

\displaystyle\leq(\log d)^{-2L}

Then with probability at least
1-\delta
over the random initialisation, the following holds for all
t_{0}
.
If all of the matrices do not change a lot for all
0\leq t<t_{0}
, i.e.,

\displaystyle\left\|W^{(l)}(t)-W^{(l)}(0)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\omega\sqrt{d},

\displaystyle\left\|W^{(l)}(t)-W^{(l)}(0)\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\begin{cases}\infty&\text{ if }l=L+1,\\
\omega\sqrt{d}\log d&\text{ if }2\leq l\leq L,\\
\omega\log d&\text{ if }l=1,\end{cases}

\displaystyle\left\|W^{(l)}(t)-W^{(l)}(0)\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\begin{cases}\infty&\text{ if }l=1\\
\omega\sqrt{d}\log d&\text{ if }2\leq l\leq L,\\
\omega\log d&\text{ if }l=L+1,\end{cases}

then we have linear convergence for
0\leq t\leq t_{0}
, i.e.,

\mathcal{L}(\theta_{t})\leq\mathcal{L}(\theta_{0})\exp\left(-\frac{\kappa^{2}\lambda\lambda_{0}t}{N}\right).

Proof.

By the assumption on
d
, with Theorem
4.3
, we can assume that the finite JNTK at initialisation is close to its limit, i.e.,

\left\|\Theta_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(1:N)},x^{(1:N)})-\kappa^{2}\Theta(x^{(1:N)},x^{(1:N)})\right\|_{F}\leq\frac{\kappa^{2}\lambda\lambda_{0}}{4}

with probability at least
1-\delta/2
.

Also from our assumption and Theorem
4.5
, we can show that the finite JNTK stays close to its initialisation,

\left\|\Theta_{d,\theta_{t}}(x^{(1:N)},x^{(1:N)})-\Theta_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(1:N)},x^{(1:N)})\right\|_{F}\leq\frac{\kappa^{2}\lambda\lambda_{0}}{4}

with probability at least
1-\delta/2
.

Combining these two, we can show that the finite JNTK during training is close to its limit,

\left\|\Theta_{d,\theta_{t}}(x^{(1:N)},x^{(1:N)})-\kappa^{2}\Theta(x^{(1:N)},x^{(1:N)})\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{\kappa^{2}\lambda\lambda_{0}}{2}

with probability at least
1-\delta
.
From the assumption
\lambda<1
, this also applies to the JNTK with multipliers,

\left\|\Lambda^{\oplus N}\Theta_{d,\theta_{t}}(x^{(1:N)},x^{(1:N)})\Lambda^{\oplus N}-\kappa^{2}\Theta_{\lambda}(x^{(1:N)},x^{(1:N)})\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{\kappa^{2}\lambda\lambda_{0}}{2}.

Now using the bound on the smallest eigenvalue, the JNTK with
\lambda
multiplier has the smallest eigenvalue bounded below as

\lambda_{\min}\left(\kappa^{2}\Theta_{\lambda}(x^{(1:N)},x^{(1:N)})\right)\geq\kappa^{2}\left(\min_{i\in[N(d_{0}+1)]}\Lambda_{i-1,i-1}^{\oplus N}\right)^{2}\lambda_{\min}\left(\Theta(x^{(1:N)},x^{(1:N)})\right)=\kappa^{2}\lambda\lambda_{0},

using the fact that for two PSD matrix
P,Q\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times n}
, the following inequality holds:

\lambda_{\min}(P\odot Q)\geq\min_{i\in[n]}P_{i-1,i-1}\lambda_{\min}(Q).

This allows the application of Weyl’s inequality to show that the minimum eigenvalue during the training is also bounded from below,

\lambda_{\min}\left(\Lambda^{\oplus N}\Theta_{d,\theta_{t}}(x^{(1:N)},x^{(1:N)})\Lambda^{\oplus N}\right)\geq\frac{\kappa^{2}\lambda\lambda_{0}}{2}.

Now let’s define a vector
u_{t}\in\mathbb{R}^{N(d_{0}+1)}
which is defined by stacking

\left[f_{d,\theta_{t}}(x^{(i)}),\sqrt{\lambda}J(f_{d,\theta_{t}})(x^{(i)})_{0},\cdots,\sqrt{\lambda}J(f_{d,\theta_{t}})(x^{(i)})_{d_{0}-1}\right].

Then, the loss function can be written in terms of
\mathcal{L}(\theta_{t})=\frac{1}{2N}\|u_{t}-\mathbf{y}^{(1:N)}\|_{2}^{2}
.
Now the time derivative of the objective function can be written as

\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{L}(\theta_{t})=\frac{d}{dt}\frac{1}{2N}\left\|u_{t}-\mathbf{y}^{(1:N)}\right\|_{2}^{2}=\frac{1}{N}\left\langle u_{t}-\mathbf{y}^{(1:N)},\dot{u}_{t}\right\rangle

(51)

Now from Lemma
4.2
, the time derivative of a function is written by
\dot{u}_{t}=-\frac{1}{N}\Lambda^{\oplus N}\Theta_{d,\theta_{t}}(x^{(1:N)},x^{(1:N)})(u_{t}-\mathbf{y}^{(1:N)})
, so substituting this in (
51
) gives

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{L}(\theta_{t})

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{N^{2}}\left\langle u_{t}-\mathbf{y}^{(1:N)},\Lambda^{\oplus N}\Theta_{d,\theta_{t}}(x^{(1:N)},x^{(1:N)})\Lambda^{\oplus N}(u_{t}-\mathbf{y}^{(1:N)})\right\rangle

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{N^{2}}(u_{t}-\mathbf{y}^{(1:N)})^{\intercal}\left(\Lambda^{\oplus N}\Theta_{d,\theta_{t}}(x^{(1:N)},x^{(1:N)})\Lambda^{\oplus N}\right)(u_{t}-\mathbf{y}^{(1:N)})

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\kappa^{2}\lambda\lambda_{0}}{2N^{2}}\|u_{t}-\mathbf{y}^{(1:N)}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{\kappa^{2}\lambda\lambda_{0}}{N}\mathcal{L}(\theta_{t}).

Then by Gronwall’s inequality, we prove the linear convergence,

\mathcal{L}(\theta_{t})\leq\mathcal{L}(\theta_{0})\exp\left(-\frac{\kappa^{2}\lambda\lambda_{0}t}{N}\right).

∎

Lemma J.2
.

Fix the fail probability
\delta>0
and network depth
L
. Suppose that the network width
d
and
\omega
satisfies

\displaystyle d

\displaystyle\geq\Omega(Nd_{0}\exp(L)/\delta),

\displaystyle d

\displaystyle\geq\Omega(\omega^{-2}),

\displaystyle\omega

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\frac{\lambda_{0}\lambda}{N\exp(\Omega(L))(\log d)^{3L}}\right),

\displaystyle\omega

\displaystyle\leq(\log d)^{-2L}.

Also suppose that at initialisation, all outputs and their Jacobian are bounded by 1, i.e., for all
i\in[N]
,

\left|f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})\right|,\left\|J(f_{d,\theta_{0}})(x^{(i)})\right\|_{\infty}\leq 1.

With probability at least
1-\delta
over the random initialisation, the following holds for all
t_{0}
and for any
l
,
if for all
0\leq t\leq t_{0}
,

•

Linear convergence happens:

\mathcal{L}(\theta_{t})\leq\mathcal{L}(\theta_{0})\exp\left(-\frac{\kappa^{2}\lambda\lambda_{0}t}{N}\right).

•

All other matrices stay close to their initialisation:

\displaystyle\left\|W^{(l^{\prime})}(t)-W^{(l^{\prime})}(0)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\omega\sqrt{d},

\displaystyle\left\|W^{(l^{\prime})}(t)-W^{(l^{\prime})}(0)\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\begin{cases}\infty&\text{ if }l^{\prime}=L+1,\\
\omega\sqrt{d}\log d&\text{ if }2\leq l^{\prime}\leq L,\\
\omega\log d&\text{ if }l^{\prime}=1,\end{cases}

\displaystyle\left\|W^{(l^{\prime})}(t)-W^{(l^{\prime})}(0)\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\begin{cases}\infty&\text{ if }l^{\prime}=1,\\
\omega\sqrt{d}\log d&\text{ if }2\leq l^{\prime}\leq L,\\
\omega\log d&\text{ if }l^{\prime}=L+1,\end{cases}

for all
l^{\prime}\in[L+1]\setminus\{l\}
.

then the chosen layer’s weight also stays close to its initialisation,

\displaystyle\left\|W^{(l)}(t)-W^{(l)}(0)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\omega\sqrt{d},

\displaystyle\left\|W^{(l)}(t)-W^{(l)}(0)\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\begin{cases}\infty&\text{ if }l=L+1,\\
\omega\sqrt{d}\log d&\text{ if }2\leq l\leq L,\\
\omega\log d&\text{ if }l=1,\end{cases}

\displaystyle\left\|W^{(l)}(t)-W^{(l)}(0)\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\begin{cases}\infty&\text{ if }l=1,\\
\omega\sqrt{d}\log d&\text{ if }2\leq l\leq L,\\
\omega\log d&\text{ if }l=L+1.\end{cases}

Proof.

We will first prove the bound for the Frobenius norm.

Let
C_{0},C_{1},C_{2}
be some constant.

\displaystyle\left\|W^{(l)}(t_{0})-W^{(l)}(0)\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{0}^{t_{0}}\frac{dW^{(l)}(t)}{dt}dt\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{0}^{t_{0}}\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\theta_{t})}{\partial W^{(l)}(t)}dt\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t_{0}}\Bigg\|\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\Bigg((f_{d,\theta_{t}}(x^{(i)})-y^{(i)})\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{t}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(l)}(t)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\lambda\sum_{\alpha=1}^{d_{0}}J(f_{d,\theta_{t}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{t}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(l)}(t)}\Bigg)\Bigg\|dt

We can define matrix
M\in\mathbb{R}^{(d_{l-1}d_{l})\times N(d_{0}+1)}
by stacking all flattened gradients w.r.t. matrix
W^{(l)}
.
Then the Frobenius norm can be rewritten and bounded as

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\left\|M\mathbf{y}^{(1:N)}\right\|\leq\frac{1}{N}\|M\|\|\mathbf{y}^{(1:N)}\|\leq\frac{\|\mathbf{y}^{(1:N)}\|}{N}\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{N(1+d_{0})}\|M_{-,k}\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\|\mathbf{y}^{(1:N)}\|}{N}\sqrt{N(1+d_{0})\max_{k}\|M_{-,k}\|^{2}}=\frac{\sqrt{1+d_{0}}}{\sqrt{N}}\max_{k}\|M_{-,i}\|\|\mathbf{y}^{(1:N)}\|

and the last term has the following form when written with the original notations,

\sqrt{\frac{1+d_{0}}{N}}\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in[N]\\
\alpha\in[d_{0}]\end{subarray}}\left\{\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{t}}(x^{(i)},\theta_{t})}{\partial W^{(l)}(t)}\right\|,\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{t}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(l)}(t)}\right\|\right\}\sqrt{\mathcal{L}(\theta_{t})}

and using
\lambda<1
, we can finally bound this by

\sqrt{1+d_{0}}\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in[N]\\
\alpha\in[d_{0}]\end{subarray}}\left\{\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{t}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(l)}(t)}\right\|,\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{t}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(l)}(t)}\right\|\right\}\sqrt{\mathcal{L}(\theta_{t})}.

Then we can bound the integral using this as

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{1+d_{0}}\int_{0}^{t_{0}}\max_{i,\alpha}\left\{\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{t}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(l)}(t)}\right\|,\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{t}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(l)}(t)}\right\|\right\}\sqrt{\mathcal{L}(\theta_{t})}dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{1+d_{0}}\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in[N]\\
0\leq\tau\leq t_{0}\end{subarray}}\left\{\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{\tau}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(l)}(\tau)}\right\|,\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{\tau}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(l)}(\tau)}\right\|\right\}\int_{0}^{t_{0}}\sqrt{\mathcal{L}(\theta_{t})}dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{1+d_{0}}\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in[N]\\
0\leq\tau\leq t_{0}\end{subarray}}\left\{\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{\tau}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(l)}(\tau)}\right\|,\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{\tau}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(l)}(\tau)}\right\|\right\}\int_{0}^{t_{0}}\sqrt{\mathcal{L}(\theta_{0})}\exp\left(-\frac{\kappa^{2}\lambda\lambda_{0}t}{2N}\right)dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2\sqrt{(1+d_{0})\mathcal{L}(\theta_{0})}}{N\kappa^{2}\lambda\lambda_{0}}\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in[N]\\
0\leq\tau\leq t_{0}\end{subarray}}\left\{\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{\tau}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(l)}(\tau)}\right\|,\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{\tau}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(l)}(\tau)}\right\|\right\}

(52)

so it is enough to bound each term at maximum.
To use our previous analysis, let’s instead bound these terms:

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{\tau}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(l)}(\tau)}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{\tau}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(l)}(0)}\right\|+\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{\tau}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(l)}(\tau)}-\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(l)}(0)}\right\|,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{\tau}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(l)}(\tau)}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{\tau}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(l)}(0)}\right\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{\tau}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(l)}(\tau)}-\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{0}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(l)}(0)}\right\|.

For the gradients at initialisation, we can bound them as

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{\tau}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(l)}(0)}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{C_{l-1}}}\left\|h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\right\|\leq\kappa O(\exp(L)).

from Lemma
I.2
and
I.4
.
Similarly, we can bound the gradient of the directional derivative as

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{0}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(l)}(0)}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{C_{l-1}}}\left\|\frac{\partial h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}^{(i)}}\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\right\|

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{C_{l-1}}}\left\|h_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})\right\|\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{0}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\kappa\exp(O(L)),

(53)

using Lemma
I.3
and
I.5
.

We can do similar analysis for the
p
-norm for
p=1,\infty
, as

\displaystyle\left\|W^{(l)}(t_{0})-W^{(l)}(0)\right\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t_{0}}\Bigg\|\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\Bigg((f_{d,\theta_{t}}(x^{(i)})-y^{(i)})\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{t}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(l)}(t)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\lambda\sum_{\alpha=1}^{d_{0}}J(f_{d,\theta_{t}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{t}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(l)}(t)}\Bigg)\Bigg\|_{p}dt,

and applying the following inequality

\displaystyle\Bigg\|\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\Bigg((f_{d,\theta_{t}}(x^{(i)})-y^{(i)})\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{t}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(l)}(t)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad+\lambda\sum_{\alpha=1}^{d_{0}}J(f_{d,\theta_{t}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{t}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(l)}(t)}\Bigg)\Bigg\|_{p}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\Bigg(|f_{d,\theta_{t}}(x^{(i)})-y^{(i)}|\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{t}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(l)}(t)}\right\|_{p}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\lambda\sum_{\alpha=1}^{d_{0}}|J(f_{d,\theta_{t}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}|\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{t}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(l)}(t)}\right\|_{p}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\mathcal{L}(\theta_{t})}\max_{\begin{subarray}{c}i\in[N]\\
\alpha\in[d_{0}]\end{subarray}}\left\{\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{t}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(l)}(t)}\right\|_{p},\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{t}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(l)}(t)}\right\|_{p}\right\}.

Again, we will decompose these matrix
p
-norms with triangle inequality, which requires us to bound the following four quantities,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(l)}(0)}\right\|_{p},

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{\tau}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(l)}(\tau)}-\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(l)}(0)}\right\|_{p},

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{0}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(l)}(0)}\right\|_{p},

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{\tau}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(l)}(\tau)}-\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{0}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(l)}(0)}\right\|_{p}.

This time, the bounds need to consider the corner cases,
l=L+1
for
p=1
and
l=1
for
p=\infty
.
We will first bound the bounds at initialisations for
p=1
, for
1\leq l\leq L
,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(l)}(0)}\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{C_{l-1}}}\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}}\right\|_{1}\left\|h_{0}^{(l-1)}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}}\right\|\left\|h_{0}^{(l-1)}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\kappa\sqrt{\log d}\exp(O(L)),

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(L+1)}(0)}\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial g_{0}^{(L+1)}}\right\|\left\|h_{0}^{(L)}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\kappa\exp(O(L))\sqrt{\frac{\log d}{d}}.

The
p=\infty
case is similar, for
2\leq l\leq L+1
,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(l)}(0)}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{C_{l-1}}}\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}}\left(h_{0}^{(l-1)}\right)^{\intercal}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{C_{l-1}}}\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}}\right\|_{\infty}\left\|h_{0}^{(l-1)}\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial g_{0}^{(l)}}\right\|_{\infty}\left\|h_{0}^{(l-1)}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{d}\cdot\frac{\kappa\sqrt{\log d}}{\sqrt{d}}O(\exp(L))

\displaystyle\leq\kappa\sqrt{\log d}\exp(O(L)),

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(1)}(0)}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial g_{0}^{(1)}}\right\|_{\infty}\left\|x^{(i)}\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\kappa\sqrt{\frac{\log d}{d}}\exp(O(L)).

and similarly, the gradient of Jacobians w.r.t weights can be bounded as

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{0}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(l)}(0)}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\kappa O((\log d)^{3/2}\exp(L)).

For the second term, we will apply a contradiction argument.
To apply Theorem
4.5
, we require the
W^{(l)}
to be close to its initialisation also, but the assumptions do not contain it.
To address this problem, let’s define the first violation time
t^{\prime}
as

t^{\prime}\coloneqq\argmin_{0\leq t\leq t_{0}}\left\{\begin{aligned} \left\|W^{(l)}(t)-W^{(l)}(0)\right\|&\geq\omega\sqrt{d},\\
\left\|W^{(l)}(t)-W^{(l)}(0)\right\|_{\infty}&\geq\begin{cases}\infty&\text{ if }l=L+1,\\
\omega\sqrt{d}\log d&\text{ if }2\leq l\leq L,\\
\omega\log d&\text{ if }l=1,\end{cases}\\
\left\|W^{(l)}(t)-W^{(l)}(0)\right\|_{1}&\geq\begin{cases}\infty&\text{ if }l=1,\\
\omega\sqrt{d}\log d&\text{ if }2\leq l\leq L,\\
\omega\log d&\text{ if }l=L+1,\end{cases}\end{aligned}\right\}.

Let’s suppose that the first condition is violated.

Then we can see that for all times
0\leq\tau<t^{\prime}
, all weights stay close to their initialisation: for all
l\in[L+1]
,

\displaystyle\left\|W^{(l)}(t)-W^{(l)}(0)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\omega\sqrt{d},

\displaystyle\left\|W^{(l)}(t)-W^{(l)}(0)\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\begin{cases}\omega\sqrt{d}\log d&\text{ if }l\geq 2,\\
\omega\log d&\text{ if }l=1,\end{cases}

\displaystyle\left\|W^{(l)}(t)-W^{(l)}(0)\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\begin{cases}\omega\sqrt{d}\log d&\text{ if }l\geq L,\\
\omega\log d&\text{ if }l=L+1.\end{cases}

By Lemma
I.8
,
I.12
,
I.10

I.14
, we can show that for all
i\in[N]
,
\alpha\in[d_{0}]
, and
l\in[L+1]
,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(l)}(t^{\prime})}-\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(l)}(0)}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{C_{l-1}}}\left(\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{t^{\prime}}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}-\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{0}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\right)\left(g_{t^{\prime}}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})\right)^{\intercal}\right\|

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{C_{l-1}}}\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{0}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\left(g_{t^{\prime}}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})-g_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})\right)^{\intercal}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{C_{l-1}}}\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{t^{\prime}}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}-\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{0}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\right\|\left\|g_{t^{\prime}}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})\right\|

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{C_{l-1}}}\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{0}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\right\|\left\|g_{t^{\prime}}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})-g_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\kappa\omega\exp(O(L)),

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(l)}(t^{\prime})}-\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{0}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(l)}(0)}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{C_{l-1}}}\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial h_{t^{\prime}}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\left(g_{t^{\prime}}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})\right)^{\intercal}-\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{0}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial h_{0}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\left(g_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})\right)^{\intercal}\right\|

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{C_{l-1}}}\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{t^{\prime}}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\left(\frac{\partial g_{t^{\prime}}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}^{(i)}}\right)^{\intercal}-\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{0}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\left(\frac{\partial g_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}^{(i)}}\right)^{\intercal}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\kappa\omega\exp(O(L))\log d.

By our assumption on
\omega
, we can show that all these terms are also
\kappa\exp(O(L))
, thereby bounding the entire term by

\left\|W^{(l)}(t^{\prime})-W^{(l)}(0)\right\|\leq O\left(\frac{\sqrt{(1+d_{0})\mathcal{L}(\theta_{0})}}{N\kappa\lambda\lambda_{0}}\right)\exp(O(L)).

From our assumption that all outputs of the function and their Jacobian are in
[-1,1]
with high probability, we can assume that
\mathcal{L}(\theta_{0})\leq 2(1+d_{0})
.

However, from our assumption of
d\geq O(1/\omega^{2})
, by increasing
d
large enough, the entire term becomes smaller than
\omega\sqrt{d}
.

This contradicts our assumption, which is the existence of the violation point
t^{\prime}
, showing that such a violation never happens.

Therefore for every
0\leq t<t_{0}
,

\left\|W^{(l)}(t)-W^{(l)}(0)\right\|\leq\sqrt{d}\omega.

Now let’s suppose that the second condition is violated. We will similarly prove this as we’ve done for the operator norm.

Using a similar approach as the operator norm, we obtain the following bounds for
2\leq l\leq L
,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(l)}(t^{\prime})}-\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(l)}(0)}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{C_{l-1}}}\left(\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{t^{\prime}}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}-\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{0}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\right)\left(g_{t^{\prime}}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})\right)^{\intercal}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{C_{l-1}}}\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{0}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\left(g_{t^{\prime}}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})-g_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})\right)^{\intercal}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{C_{l-1}}}\left\|\frac{f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{t^{\prime}}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}-\frac{f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{0}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\right\|_{\infty}\left\|g_{t^{\prime}}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{C_{l-1}}}\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{0}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\right\|_{\infty}\left\|g_{t^{\prime}}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})-g_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\kappa\omega\exp(O(L))(\log d)^{2L}+\kappa\omega\exp(O(L))\sqrt{\log d},

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(1)}(t^{\prime})}-\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(1)}(0)}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{t^{\prime}}^{(1)}(x^{(i)})}-\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{0}^{(1)}(x^{(i)})}\right)\left(x^{(i)}\right)^{\intercal}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\kappa\omega\exp(O(L))\frac{(\log d)^{2L}}{\sqrt{d}},

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(l)}(t^{\prime})}-\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{0}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(l)}(0)}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{C_{l-1}}}\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{t^{\prime}}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\left(\frac{\partial g_{t^{\prime}}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}^{(i)}}\right)^{\intercal}-\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{0}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\left(\frac{\partial g_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}^{(i)}}\right)^{\intercal}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{C_{l-1}}}\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial h_{t^{\prime}}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\left(g_{t^{\prime}}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})\right)^{\intercal}-\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{0}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial h_{0}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\left(g_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})\right)^{\intercal}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\kappa\omega\exp(O(L))(\log d)^{2L}+\kappa\omega\exp(O(L))(\log d)^{3L+1},

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(1)}(t^{\prime})}-\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{0}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(1)}(0)}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{t^{\prime}}^{(1)}(x^{(i)})}-\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{0}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\right)e_{\alpha-1}^{\intercal}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|\left(\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial h_{t^{\prime}}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}-\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{0}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial h_{0}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\right)\left(x^{(i)}\right)^{\intercal}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{t^{\prime}}^{(1)}(x^{(i)})}-\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{0}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\right)\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|\left(\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial h_{t^{\prime}}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}-\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{0}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial h_{0}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\right)\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\kappa\omega\exp(O(L))\frac{(\log d)^{3L+1}}{\sqrt{d}}.

As we’ve done in the operator norm cases, we can set
\omega\leq(\log d)^{-3L}
so that the norms at time
t=0
dominate the perturbed norm, showing that

\displaystyle\left\|W^{(l)}(t^{\prime})-W^{(l)}(0)\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\frac{d_{0}}{N\kappa\lambda\lambda_{0}}\right)\exp(O(L))\log d,

\displaystyle\left\|W^{(1)}(t^{\prime})-W^{(1)}(0)\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\frac{d_{0}}{N\kappa\lambda\lambda_{0}}\right)\exp(O(L))\frac{\log d}{\sqrt{d}},

and setting
d\geq\Omega(1/\omega^{2})
makes these terms to be bounded by
\omega\sqrt{d}\log d,\omega\log d
, respectively.
This results in a contradiction, showing that the second condition can not be violated.

Now we finally consider the 1-norm. For
2\leq l\leq L
,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(l)}(t^{\prime})}-\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(l)}(0)}\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{C_{l-1}}}\left(\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{t^{\prime}}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}-\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{0}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\right)\left(g_{t^{\prime}}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})\right)^{\intercal}\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|\frac{1}{\sqrt{C_{l-1}}}\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{0}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\left(g_{t^{\prime}}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})-g_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})\right)^{\intercal}\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{C_{l-1}}}\left\|\frac{f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{t^{\prime}}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}-\frac{f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{0}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\right\|_{1}\left\|g_{t^{\prime}}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\sqrt{C_{l-1}}}\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{0}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\right\|_{1}\left\|g_{t^{\prime}}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})-g_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\kappa\omega\exp(O(L))\log d+\kappa\omega\exp(O(L))(\log d)^{L},

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(L+1)}(t^{\prime})}-\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial W^{(L+1)}(0)}\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{C_{l-1}}}\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{0}^{(L+1)}(x^{(i)})}\right\|_{1}\left\|g_{t^{\prime}}^{(L)}(x^{(i)})-g_{0}^{(L)}(x^{(i)})\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\kappa\exp(O(L))\frac{(\log d)^{L}}{\sqrt{d}},

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(l)}(t^{\prime})}-\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{0}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(l)}(0)}\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{C_{l-1}}}\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{t^{\prime}}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\left(\frac{\partial g_{t^{\prime}}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}^{(i)}}\right)^{\intercal}-\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{0}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\left(\frac{\partial g_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}^{(i)}}\right)^{\intercal}\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{C_{l-1}}}\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial h_{t^{\prime}}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\left(g_{t^{\prime}}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})\right)^{\intercal}-\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{0}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial h_{0}^{(l)}(x^{(i)})}\left(g_{0}^{(l-1)}(x^{(i)})\right)^{\intercal}\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\kappa\omega\exp(O(L))(\log d)^{2L}+\kappa\omega\exp(O(L))(\log d)^{L},

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(L+1)}(t^{\prime})}-\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{0}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial W^{(L+1)}(0)}\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}\left\|\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{t^{\prime}}^{(L+1)}(x^{(i)})}\left(\frac{\partial g_{t^{\prime}}^{(L)}(x^{(i)})}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}^{(i)}}\right)^{\intercal}-\frac{\partial f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{(i)})}{\partial h_{0}^{(L+1)}(x^{(i)})}\left(\frac{\partial g_{0}^{(L)}(x^{(i)})}{\partial x_{\alpha-1}^{(i)}}\right)^{\intercal}\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{d}}\left\|\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{t^{\prime}}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial h_{t^{\prime}}^{(L+1)}(x^{(i)})}\left(g_{t^{\prime}}^{(L)}(x^{(i)})\right)^{\intercal}-\frac{\partial J(f_{d,\theta_{0}})(x^{(i)})_{\alpha-1}}{\partial h_{0}^{(L+1)}(x^{(i)})}\left(g_{0}^{(L)}(x^{(i)})\right)^{\intercal}\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\kappa\omega\exp(O(L))\frac{(\log d)^{2L}}{\sqrt{d}}+\kappa\omega\exp(O(L))\frac{(\log d)^{L}}{\sqrt{d}}.

Again setting
\omega\leq(\log d)^{-3L}
allow us to bound the entires term by norm at
t=0
, showing that

\displaystyle\left\|W^{(l)}(t^{\prime})-W^{(l)}(0)\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\frac{d_{0}}{N\kappa\lambda\lambda_{0}}\right)\exp(O(L))\log d,

\displaystyle\left\|W^{(L+1)}(t^{\prime})-W^{(L+1)}(0)\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\frac{d_{0}}{N\kappa\lambda\lambda_{0}}\right)\exp(O(L))\frac{\log d}{\sqrt{d}}.

Setting
d\geq\Omega(1/\omega^{2})
makes these terms to be bounded by
\omega\sqrt{d}\log d,\omega\log d
, respectively.

All three conditions can not happen, and this proves our result.
∎

Now we are ready to prove Theorem
4.5
.
We first give a detailed statement of this theorem.

Theorem J.3
.

Fix the fail probability
\delta
, and error
\epsilon
.
Suppose that Assumption
4.4
holds with the smallest eigenvalue
\lambda_{0}
.

Suppose that the width
d
, the coefficient
\kappa
, and the threshold
\omega
satisfy

\displaystyle\kappa

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\log(8N(1+d_{0})/\delta)},

\displaystyle d

\displaystyle\geq F_{1}\left(x^{(1:N)},L,\delta,\frac{\epsilon}{\kappa}\right),

\displaystyle d

\displaystyle\geq F_{2}\left(x^{(1:N)},L,\delta,\epsilon\right),

\displaystyle d

\displaystyle\geq\Omega(Nd_{0}\exp(L)/\delta),

\displaystyle d

\displaystyle\geq\Omega(\omega^{-2}),

\displaystyle\omega

\displaystyle\leq O\left(\frac{\lambda_{0}\lambda}{N\exp(\Omega(L))(\log d)^{3L}}\right),

\displaystyle\omega

\displaystyle\leq(\log d)^{-3L}

Then with probability at least
1-\delta
, for all
t\geq 0
,

\left\|\Theta_{d,\theta_{t}}(x^{(i)},x^{(j)})-\kappa^{2}\Theta(x^{(i)},x^{(j)})\right\|_{F}\leq\kappa^{2}\epsilon.

for all
i,j\in[N]
.

Moreover, linear convergence happens:

\mathcal{L}(\theta_{t})\leq\exp\left(-\frac{\kappa^{2}\lambda\lambda_{0}t}{N}\right)\mathcal{L}(\theta_{0}).

Proof.

From the assumption of
\kappa
, we can show that

P\left(\forall i\in[N].\left\|g(x^{(i)})\right\|_{\infty}\leq 1\right)\geq 1-\frac{\delta}{4}

where
g\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\kappa^{2}\Sigma^{(L)})
, and together with the first assumption on
d
, the network outputs and their Jacobian are bounded by 1 at initialisation with probability at least
1-\delta/2
.

We will first prove that the linear convergence and the weights stay close to their initialisation.
This can be proven by contradiction argument with Lemma
J.2
and
J.1
.

Suppose that either linear convergence does not happen,

\mathcal{L}(\theta_{t})>\mathcal{L}(\theta_{0})\exp\left(-\frac{\kappa^{2}\lambda\lambda_{0}t}{N}\right)

or one of the weights becomes far from its initialisation,

\displaystyle\left\|W^{(l)}(t)-W^{(l)}(0)\right\|

\displaystyle>\omega\sqrt{d},

\displaystyle\left\|W^{(l)}(t)-W^{(l)}(0)\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle>\begin{cases}\omega\sqrt{d}\log d&\text{ if }l\geq 2,\\
\omega\log d&\text{ if }l=1,\end{cases}

\displaystyle\left\|W^{(l)}(t)-W^{(l)}(0)\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle>\begin{cases}\omega\sqrt{d}\log d&\text{ if }l\geq L,\\
\omega\log d&\text{ if }l=L+1.\end{cases}

Write
\tau
as the first violation time that this happens,

\tau=\argmin_{0\leq t}\left\{t:\text{Linear Convergence fail or weight is far from init.}\right\}.

Then by definition, the linear convergence is satisfied, and the weights are close to its initialisation for all
0\leq t<\tau
.
Now Lemma
J.1
shows that at time
\tau
, the linear convergence happens, and similarly, Lemma
J.2
show that the weights at time
\tau
also stay close to its initialisation.
This is a contradiction, which shows that our assumption, of the existence of a violation point is false.

So now we can assume that

\displaystyle\left\|W^{(l)}(t)-W^{(l)}(0)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\omega\sqrt{d},

\displaystyle\left\|W^{(l)}(t)-W^{(l)}(0)\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\begin{cases}\omega\sqrt{d}\log d&\text{ if }l\geq 2,\\
\omega\log d&\text{ if }l=1,\end{cases}

\displaystyle\left\|W^{(l)}(t)-W^{(l)}(0)\right\|_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\begin{cases}\omega\sqrt{d}\log d&\text{ if }l\geq L,\\
\omega\log d&\text{ if }l=L+1.\end{cases}

for all
l\in[L+1]
and
t\geq 0
.
Now by Theorem
J.3
, we can show that

\left\|\Theta_{d,\theta_{t}}(x^{(i)},x^{(j)})-\kappa^{2}\Theta(x^{(i)},x^{(j)})\right\|_{F}\leq\kappa^{2}\epsilon

for all time
t\geq 0
,
i,j\in[N]
.
∎

Appendix K
Proof of Theorem
4.6

Before presenting the proof, we need several additional definitions to be used in the proof.
We define the feature function of limiting JNTK
\phi(x)
, which satisfies

\phi(x)^{\intercal}\phi(x^{\prime})=\Theta(x,x^{\prime})

with
\phi_{0}(x),\ldots,\phi_{d_{0}}(t)
the columns of this feature function.

We then define the limiting JNTK regressor as

f_{\mathrm{ntk}}(x;\vartheta)\coloneqq\kappa\Lambda\phi(x)^{\intercal}\vartheta\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{0}+1}

for trainable
\vartheta
, and define
u_{\mathrm{ntk}}(\vartheta)\in\mathbb{R}^{N(1+d_{0})}
by stacking
f_{\mathrm{ntk}}(x^{(i)};\vartheta)
for
i\in[N]
.

If we consider the training of
\vartheta
under the same objective (1), which is reformulated as

\mathcal{L}(\vartheta)\coloneqq\frac{1}{2N}\left\|u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\vartheta)-\mathbf{y}\right\|_{2}^{2}

when initialised with
\vartheta_{0}=0
, the solution is given by

\vartheta_{\infty}=\argmin_{\vartheta}\left\|\vartheta\right\|_{2}^{2},\text{ such that }u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\vartheta)=\mathbf{y}.

Note that this optimal parameter
\vartheta_{\infty}
gives the solution
f_{\mathrm{ntk}}
defined (5).

We also define
f_{\mathrm{nn}}
similarly,

f_{\mathrm{nn}}(x;\theta)\coloneqq\left(f_{d,\theta}(x;\theta),\sqrt{\lambda}J(f_{d,\theta})(x;\theta)_{0},\ldots,\sqrt{\lambda}J(f_{d,\theta})(x;\theta)_{d_{0}-1}\right)^{\intercal}\in\mathbb{R}^{1+d_{0}}

and
u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta)\in\mathbb{R}^{N(d_{0}+1)}
by stacking
f_{\mathrm{nn}}(x^{(i)};\theta)
for
i\in[N]
.

Now to track the dynamics of the limiting JNTK regressor, we will take the time derivative.

\displaystyle\frac{df_{\mathrm{ntk}}(x;\vartheta_{t})}{dt}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\partial f_{\mathrm{ntk}}(x;\vartheta_{t})}{\partial\vartheta_{t}}\right)^{\intercal}\left(\frac{d\vartheta_{t}}{dt}\right)

\displaystyle=\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{\mathrm{ntk}}(x;\vartheta)}{\partial\vartheta_{t}},-\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\vartheta_{t})}{\partial\vartheta_{t}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=\left\langle\kappa\phi(t),-\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\vartheta_{t})}{\partial\vartheta_{t}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{N}\left\langle\kappa\phi(t),\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(f_{\mathrm{ntk}}(x^{(i)};\vartheta_{t})-\mathbf{y}^{(i)}\right)^{\intercal}\frac{\partial f_{\mathrm{ntk}}(x^{(i)};\vartheta_{t})}{\partial\vartheta_{t}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{N}\left\langle\kappa\phi(t),\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(f_{\mathrm{ntk}}(x^{(i)};\vartheta_{t})-\mathbf{y}^{(i)}\right)^{\intercal}\kappa\phi(x^{(i)})\right\rangle

\displaystyle=-\frac{\kappa^{2}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\Theta_{\lambda}(x,x^{(i)})\left(f_{\mathrm{ntk}}(x^{(i)};\vartheta_{t})-\mathbf{y}^{(i)}\right).

Then we can see that these dynamics are nearly identical to NN’s dynamics, except that they use different kernels.

\displaystyle\frac{df_{\mathrm{nn}}(x;\theta_{t})}{dt}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\partial f_{\mathrm{nn}}(x;\theta_{t})}{\partial\theta_{t}}\right)^{\intercal}\left(\frac{d\theta_{t}}{dt}\right)

\displaystyle=\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{\mathrm{nn}}(x;\theta)}{\partial\theta_{t}},-\frac{\partial\mathcal{L}(\theta_{t})}{\partial\theta_{t}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{N}\left\langle\frac{\partial f_{\mathrm{nn}}(x;\theta)}{\partial\theta_{t}},\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(f_{\mathrm{nn}}(x^{(i)};\vartheta_{t})-y^{(i)}\right)^{\intercal}\frac{\partial f_{\mathrm{nn}}(x^{(i)};\theta_{t})}{\partial\theta_{t}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\Lambda\Theta_{d,\theta_{t}}(x,x^{(i)})\Lambda\left(f_{\mathrm{nn}}(x^{(i)};\theta_{t})-\mathbf{y}^{(i)}\right).

So to analyse the difference between limiting JNTK regressor and NN, we can bound them using the integral form

\displaystyle\left|f_{d,\theta_{\infty}}(x^{*})-f_{\mathrm{ntk}}(x^{*})\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{*})-f_{\mathrm{ntk}}(x^{*};\vartheta_{0})_{0}\right|+\left|\int_{0}^{\infty}\left(\frac{df_{\mathrm{nn}}(x^{*};\theta_{t})_{0}}{dt}-\frac{df_{\mathrm{ntk}}(x^{*};\vartheta_{t})_{0}}{dt}\right)\right|

First, since we initialised
\vartheta_{0}=0
, the first term is
|f_{d,\theta_{0}}(x^{*})|
which is bounded by
\epsilon_{0}
with our assumption, so it is enough to show that the integral term is smaller than
\epsilon_{0}
.

Using our previous expansion of time derivative, we can rewrite the integral as

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{\infty}\left(\frac{df_{\mathrm{nn}}(x^{*};\theta_{t})_{0}}{dt}-\frac{df_{\mathrm{ntk}}(x^{*};\vartheta_{t})_{0}}{dt}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg|-\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\int_{0}^{\infty}\bigg(\Lambda\Theta_{d,\theta_{t}}(x^{*},x^{(i)})\Lambda\left(f_{\mathrm{nn}}(x^{(i)};\theta_{t})-\mathbf{y}^{(i)}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\quad-\kappa^{2}\Theta_{\lambda}(x^{*},x^{(i)})\left(f_{\mathrm{ntk}}(x^{(i)};\vartheta_{t})-\mathbf{y}^{(i)}\right)\bigg)_{0}dt\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{N}\int_{0}^{\infty}\left(\left(\Lambda\Theta_{d,\theta_{t}}(x^{*},x^{(i)})\Lambda-\kappa^{2}\Theta_{\lambda}(x^{*},x^{(i)})\right)\left(f_{\mathrm{nn}}(x^{(i)};\theta_{t})-\mathbf{y}^{(i)}\right)\right)_{0}dt\right|

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{N}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{N}\int_{0}^{\infty}\left(\kappa^{2}\Theta_{\lambda}(x^{*},x^{(i)})\left(f_{\mathrm{nn}}(x^{(i)};\theta_{t})-f_{\mathrm{ntk}}(x^{(i)};\vartheta_{t})\right)\right)_{0}dt\right|.

For the first term, we can bound it through

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\left|\sum_{i=1}^{N}\int_{0}^{\infty}\left(\left(\Lambda\Theta_{d,\theta_{t}}(x^{*},x^{(i)})\Lambda-\kappa^{2}\Theta_{\lambda}(x^{*},x^{(i)})\right)\left(f_{\mathrm{nn}}(x^{(i)};\theta_{t})-\mathbf{y}^{(i)}\right)\right)_{0}dt\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\max_{\tau\geq 0}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\Lambda\Theta_{d,\theta_{\tau}}(x^{*},x^{(i)})\Lambda-\kappa^{2}\Theta_{\lambda}(x^{*},x^{(i)})\right\|_{F}\right)\int_{0}^{\infty}\left\|u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{t})-\mathbf{y}\right\|dt.

By Theorem
J.3
, we can let
d
large enough so that

\left\|\Lambda\Theta_{d,\theta_{\tau}}(x^{*},x^{(i)})\Lambda-\kappa^{2}\Theta_{\lambda}(x^{*},x^{(i)})\right\|_{F}\leq\kappa^{2}\epsilon_{\Theta}

for all
\tau\geq 0
and
i\in[N]
, for some
\epsilon_{\Theta}
that will be choosen later.
And from the same Theorem, we can also show that the integral term is bounded, as follows:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\left\|u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{t})-\mathbf{y}\right\|dt

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\infty}\sqrt{2N\mathcal{L}(\theta_{t})}dt

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2N\mathcal{L}(\theta_{0})}\int_{0}^{\infty}\exp\left(-\frac{\kappa^{2}\lambda\lambda_{0}t}{2N}\right)dt

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{8N^{3}\mathcal{L}(\theta_{0})}}{\kappa^{2}\lambda\lambda_{0}}.

From Remark
E.1
, we can assume that all function outputs and their Jacobian are in
[-1,1]
, which allows us to bound
\sqrt{\mathcal{L}(\theta_{0})}\leq\sqrt{2(1+d_{0})}
.

For the second term, we will first divide this integral into two intervals
[0,t_{0}]
and
[t_{0},\infty]
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\left|\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\int_{0}^{\infty}\kappa^{2}\Theta_{\lambda}(x^{*},x^{(i)})\left(f_{\mathrm{nn}}(x^{(i)};\theta_{t})-f_{\mathrm{ntk}}(x^{(i)};\vartheta_{t})\right)\right)_{0}dt\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\kappa^{2}\Theta_{\lambda}(x^{*},x^{(i)})\right\|_{F}\right)\int_{0}^{\infty}\left\|u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{t})-u_{\mathrm{ntk}}(\vartheta_{t})\right\|dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\kappa^{2}\Theta_{\lambda}(x^{*},x^{(i)})\right\|_{F}\right)\left(\int_{t_{0}}^{\infty}\left\|u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{t})-u_{\mathrm{ntk}}(\vartheta_{t})\right\|dt+\int_{0}^{t_{0}}\left\|u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{t})-u_{\mathrm{ntk}}(\vartheta_{t})\right\|dt\right)

for some
t_{0}\geq 0
, so to prove that this term converges to zero, we should show that both integrals converge to zero.

The integral from
t_{0}
to
\infty
can be bounded with linear convergence of both predictors:

\displaystyle\int_{t_{0}}^{\infty}\left\|u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{t})-u_{\mathrm{ntk}}(\vartheta_{t})\right\|dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{t_{0}}^{\infty}\left\|u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{t})-\mathbf{y}\right\|+\left\|u_{\mathrm{ntk}}(\vartheta_{t})-\mathbf{y}\right\|dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{0}}^{\infty}\sqrt{2N\mathcal{L}(\theta_{t})}+\sqrt{2N\mathcal{L}(\vartheta_{t})}dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{t_{0}}^{\infty}\sqrt{2N\mathcal{L}(\theta_{t_{0}})}\exp\left(-\frac{\kappa^{2}\lambda\lambda_{0}(t-t_{0})}{2N}\right)+\sqrt{2N\mathcal{L}(\vartheta_{t_{0}})}\exp\left(-\frac{\kappa^{2}\lambda\lambda_{0}(t-t_{0})}{2N}\right)dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\left(\sqrt{2N\mathcal{L}(\theta_{0})}+\sqrt{2N\mathcal{L}(\vartheta_{0})}\right)\exp\left(-\frac{\kappa^{2}\lambda\lambda_{0}t_{0}}{2N}\right)\right)\frac{2N}{\kappa^{2}\lambda\lambda_{0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(4\sqrt{N(1+d_{0})}\exp\left(-\frac{\kappa^{2}\lambda\lambda_{0}t_{0}}{2N}\right)\right)\frac{2N}{\kappa^{2}\lambda\lambda_{0}}

where we used Remark
E.1
to show that
\sqrt{\mathcal{L}(\theta_{0})}\leq\sqrt{2(d_{0}+1)}
and
\vartheta_{0}=0
to show that
\sqrt{\mathcal{L}(\vartheta_{0})}\leq\sqrt{2(1+d_{0})}
.
Then setting
t_{0}

t_{0}=\frac{2N}{\kappa^{2}\lambda\lambda_{0}}\log\left(\frac{8\sqrt{N^{3}(1+d_{0})}}{\lambda\lambda_{0}\epsilon_{\Theta}}\right)

allow us to bound this integral by
\epsilon_{\Theta}/\kappa^{2}
.
Combining with
\left\|\kappa^{2}\Theta_{\lambda}(x^{*},x^{(i)})\right\|_{F}\leq\kappa^{2}(L+1)(1+d_{0})
gives resulting bound
\epsilon_{\Theta}(L+1)(1+d_{0})
.

For the rest of the integral, we can expand the difference in the integral form

\displaystyle u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{t})-u_{\mathrm{ntk}}(\vartheta_{t})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{0})+\int_{0}^{t}\frac{du_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{\tau})}{d\tau}-\frac{du_{\mathrm{ntk}}(\vartheta_{\tau})}{d\tau}d\tau.

We can continue rewriting the integral as

\displaystyle\frac{du_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{\tau})-u_{\mathrm{ntk}}(\vartheta_{\tau})}{d\tau}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\Lambda^{\oplus N}\Theta_{d,\theta_{\tau}}(x^{(1:N)},x^{(1:N)})\Lambda^{\oplus N}(u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{\tau})-\mathbf{y})

\displaystyle\quad+\kappa^{2}\Theta_{\lambda}(x^{(1:N)},x^{(1:N)})(u_{\mathrm{ntk}}(\vartheta_{\tau})-\mathbf{y})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(-\Lambda^{\oplus N}\Theta_{d,\theta_{\tau}}(x^{(1:N)},x^{(1:N)})\Lambda^{\oplus N}+\kappa^{2}\Theta_{\lambda}(x^{(1:N)},x^{(1:N)})\right)(u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{\tau})-\mathbf{y})

\displaystyle\quad-\kappa^{2}\Theta_{\lambda}(x^{(1:N)},x^{(1:N)})\left(u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{\tau})-u_{\mathrm{ntk}}(\vartheta_{\tau})\right).

From Assumption
4.4
,
\Theta_{\lambda}(x^{(1:N)},x^{(1:N)})
is positive definite, so the second term makes the
\|u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{\tau})-u_{\mathrm{ntk}}(\vartheta_{\tau})\|
decrease.
So to bound the norm we can ignore it, and focus on the first term only.

\displaystyle\left\|u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{t})-u_{\mathrm{ntk}}(\vartheta_{t})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Bigg\|u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{0})

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{t}\left(-\Lambda^{\oplus N}\Theta_{d,\theta_{\tau}}(x^{(1:N)},x^{(1:N)})\Lambda^{\oplus N}+\kappa^{2}\Theta_{\lambda}(x^{(1:N)},x^{(1:N)})\right)(u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{\tau})-\mathbf{y})d\tau\Bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\epsilon_{0}\sqrt{N(1+d_{0})}

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{0}^{t}\left\|-\Lambda^{\oplus N}\Theta_{d,\theta_{\tau}}(x^{(1:N)},x^{(1:N)})\Lambda^{\oplus N}+\kappa^{2}\Theta_{\lambda}(x^{(1:N)},x^{(1:N)})\right\|_{F}\|u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{\tau})-\mathbf{y}\|d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\epsilon_{0}\sqrt{N(1+d_{0})}

\displaystyle\quad+\max_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\|-\Lambda^{\oplus N}\Theta_{d,\theta_{\tau}}(x^{(1:N)},x^{(1:N)})\Lambda^{\oplus N}+\kappa^{2}\Theta_{\lambda}(x^{(1:N)},x^{(1:N)})\right\|_{F}\int_{0}^{t}\|u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{\tau})-\mathbf{y}\|d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\epsilon_{0}\sqrt{N(1+d_{0})}+N\kappa^{2}\epsilon_{\Theta}\int_{0}^{t}\|u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{\tau})-\mathbf{y}\|d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\epsilon_{0}\sqrt{N(1+d_{0})}+N\kappa^{2}\epsilon_{\Theta}\int_{0}^{\infty}\sqrt{2N\mathcal{L}(\theta_{\tau})}d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\epsilon_{0}\sqrt{N(1+d_{0})}+2N\kappa^{2}\epsilon_{\Theta}\sqrt{2N\mathcal{L}(\theta_{0})}\int_{0}^{\infty}\exp\left(-\frac{\kappa^{2}\lambda\lambda_{0}\tau}{2N}\right)d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\epsilon_{0}\sqrt{N(1+d_{0})}+\frac{8N^{2}\sqrt{N(1+d_{0})}\epsilon_{\Theta}}{\lambda\lambda_{0}}.

Integrating it gives

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t_{0}}\left\|u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{t})-u_{\mathrm{ntk}}(\vartheta_{t})\right\|dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\epsilon_{0}\sqrt{N(1+d_{0})}+\frac{8N^{2}\sqrt{N(1+d_{0})}\epsilon_{\Theta}}{\lambda\lambda_{0}}\right)t_{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\epsilon_{0}\sqrt{N(1+d_{0})}+\frac{8N^{2}\sqrt{N(1+d_{0})}\epsilon_{\Theta}}{\lambda\lambda_{0}}\right)\times\left(\frac{2N}{\kappa^{2}\lambda\lambda_{0}}\log\left(\frac{8\sqrt{N^{3}(1+d_{0})}}{\lambda\lambda_{0}\epsilon_{\Theta}}\right)\right).

We finally multiply
\kappa^{2}(L+1)\cdot(1+d_{0})
as we’ve done in the first integral.

Summing up, the bound is

\displaystyle\left|f_{d,\theta_{\infty}}(x^{*})-f_{\mathrm{ntk}}(x^{*})_{0}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\epsilon_{0}+\frac{4\epsilon_{\Theta}\sqrt{N^{3}(1+d_{0})}}{\lambda\lambda_{0}}+(L+1)\cdot(d_{0}+1)\cdot\epsilon_{\Theta}

\displaystyle+(L+1)\cdot(d_{0}+1)\cdot\left(\epsilon_{0}\sqrt{N(1+d_{0})}+\frac{8N^{2}\sqrt{N(1+d_{0})}\epsilon_{\Theta}}{\lambda\lambda_{0}}\right)\frac{2N}{\lambda\lambda_{0}}\log\frac{8\sqrt{N^{3}(1+d_{0})}}{\lambda\lambda_{0}\epsilon_{\Theta}}.

Appendix L
Proofs of Theorems
4.5
and
4.6
for Gradient Descent

Consider the loss evaluation at
k+1
time step with abusing notation of
u_{nn}(\theta_{k+1})
, then

\displaystyle\|u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{k+1})-\mathbf{y}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{y}-u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{k})+u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{k})-u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{k+1})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{y}-u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{k})\|_{2}^{2}-2\langle\mathbf{y}-u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{k}),u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{k+1})-u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{k})\rangle+\|u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{k+1})-u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{k})\|_{2}^{2}

so to quantify convergence, we should analyse

2\langle\mathbf{y}-u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{k}),u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{k+1})-u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{k})\rangle,\qquad\|u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{k+1})-u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{k})\|_{2}^{2}.

Now by the Taylor expansion, we can first rewrite
u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{k+1})-u_{\mathrm{nn}}(\theta_{k})
, which is also a gradient descent analogue of Lemma
4.2
,
